-- Useskills: bam skill Z/X/C/V theo loai vu khi (Elite Hunt).
Useskills = function(weapon, skill)
  if weapon == "Melee" then
    weaponSc("Melee")
    if skill == "Z" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game);
    elseif skill == "X" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game);
    elseif skill == "C" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game);
    end
  elseif weapon == "Sword" then
    weaponSc("Sword")
    if skill == "Z" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game);
    elseif skill == "X" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game);
    end
  elseif weapon == "Blox Fruit" then
    weaponSc("Blox Fruit")
    if skill == "Z" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game);
    elseif skill == "X" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game);
    elseif skill == "C" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "C", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "C", false, game);        
    elseif skill == "V" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "V", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "V", false, game);
    end
  elseif weapon == "Gun" then
    weaponSc("Gun")
    if skill == "Z" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Z", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Z", false, game);
    elseif skill == "X" then
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "X", false, game);
      vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "X", false, game);
    end
  end
  if weapon == "nil" and skill == "Y" then
    vim1:SendKeyEvent(true, "Y", false, game);
    vim1:SendKeyEvent(false, "Y", false, game);
  end
end

            repeat task.wait()
              _G.SailBoats = true
            until not _G.Dojoo or not progress
            _G.SailBoats = false
          elseif NameBelt == "Purple" then
            repeat task.wait()
              _G.FarmEliteHunt = true
            until not _G.Dojoo or not progress
            _G.FarmEliteHunt = false
          elseif NameBelt == "Red" then

              _G.FishBoat = true

            _G.SailBoats = false

            _G.Prehis_Find = false

-- ===== AUTO UPGRADE RACE V2-V3 DRACO (port tu Banana Cat Hub) =====
-- Flow goc Banana Cat (ham AutoUpgradeRaceDraco): Dragon Wizard (Hydra, Sea 3)
--   -> RF/InteractDragonQuest Speak -> V2/V3: Ascension Begin
--   -> V2InProgress: farm 5 Fire Flower (nhat tren map, het thi giet Forest Pirate de roi hoa)
--   -> V3InProgress: giet Terrorshark (thuyen -> Danger Lv 6)
--   -> TurnInReady: Ascension Complete
-- Module that, toggle chay vong lap goi Step() trong pcall (giong Banana Cat).
local DracoUpgradeModule = {}
_G.DracoUpgradeModule = DracoUpgradeModule
DracoUpgradeModule.Running = false
DracoUpgradeModule.Quest = nil
DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark = false
DracoUpgradeModule.SharkRef = nil
DracoUpgradeModule.LastNotify = 0
DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedSea = false
DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedNoWizard = false

local function DracoNotify(content)
    if tick() - DracoUpgradeModule.LastNotify < 10 then return end
    DracoUpgradeModule.LastNotify = tick()
    pcall(function()
        Window:Notify({Title = "Auto Upgrade Race V2-V3 Draco", Content = content, Image = "rbxassetid://117049078306665", Duration = 5})
    end)
end

local function DracoTween(cf)
    pcall(function()
        if cf and typeof(cf) == "CFrame" then
            if KuriTween then KuriTween(cf)
            elseif _tp then _tp(cf) end
        end
    end)
end

local function DracoHRP()
    local char = plr and plr.Character
    return char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or nil
end

local function DracoCountItem(name)
    local n = 0
    pcall(function()
        local bp = plr and plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
        if bp then
            for _, t in pairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                if t.Name == name then n = n + 1 end
            end
        end
        local char = plr and plr.Character
        if char then
            for _, t in pairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if t:IsA("Tool") and t.Name == name then n = n + 1 end
            end
        end
        local inv = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory")
        if type(inv) == "table" then
            for _, v in pairs(inv) do
                if type(v) == "table" and v.Name == name and type(v.Count) == "number" then
                    n = n + v.Count
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    return n
end

local function DracoGetDragonRF()
    local rf = nil
    pcall(function()
        local net = nil
        if replicated and replicated.Modules then net = replicated.Modules.Net end
        if not net then
            local mods = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("Modules")
            if mods then net = mods:FindFirstChild("Net") end
        end
        if net then rf = net:FindFirstChild("RF/InteractDragonQuest") end
    end)
    return rf
end

local function DracoSpeak()
    local res = nil
    pcall(function()
        local rf = DracoGetDragonRF()
        if rf then res = rf:InvokeServer({NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = "Speak"}) end
    end)
    return res
end

local function DracoAscension(action)
    pcall(function()
        local rf = DracoGetDragonRF()
        if rf then rf:InvokeServer({NPC = "Dragon Wizard", Command = "Ascension", Action = action}) end
    end)
end

local function DracoFindWizard()
    local w = nil
    pcall(function()
        local npcs = workspace:FindFirstChild("NPCs")
        if npcs then w = npcs:FindFirstChild("Dragon Wizard") end
        if not w then
            local rsNpcs = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage"):FindFirstChild("NPCs")
            if rsNpcs then w = rsNpcs:FindFirstChild("Dragon Wizard") end
        end
    end)
    return w
end

local function DracoFindFireFlower()
    local f = nil
    pcall(function()
        local ff = workspace:FindFirstChild("FireFlowers")
        if ff then
            for _, m in pairs(ff:GetChildren()) do
                if m:IsA("Model") then f = m break end
            end
        end
    end)
    return f
end

local function DracoFindShark()
    local shark = nil
    pcall(function()
        local function scan(parent)
            if not parent or shark then return end
            for _, m in pairs(parent:GetChildren()) do
                if m:IsA("Model") and m.Name == "Terrorshark" then
                    local h = m:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    if h and h.Health > 0 then shark = m return end
                end
            end
        end
        scan(workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies"))
        scan(workspace:FindFirstChild("SeaBeasts"))
        scan(workspace)
    end)
    return shark
end

local function DracoFindBoat()
    local boat, seat = nil, nil
    pcall(function()
        local boats = workspace:FindFirstChild("Boats")
        if boats then
            for _, b in pairs(boats:GetChildren()) do
                local owner = b:FindFirstChild("Owner")
                if owner and plr and tostring(owner.Value) == tostring(plr.Name) then
                    local s = b:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("VehicleSeat", true)
                    if s then boat, seat = b, s break end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    return boat, seat
end

local function DracoGoWizard(wizard)
    local hrp = DracoHRP()
    local whrp = wizard and wizard:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if hrp and whrp and (whrp.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
        DracoTween(whrp.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 4, 4))
        return true
    end
    return false
end

function DracoUpgradeModule.Step()
    if not _G.AutoUpgradeRaceDraco then return end
    if not World3 then
        if not DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedSea then
            DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedSea = true
            DracoNotify("Draco quest chi co o Sea 3 (Hydra Island)")
        end
        task.wait(5)
        return
    end
    local race = nil
    pcall(function() race = plr.Data.Race.Value end)
    if race == nil then
        task.wait(2)
        return
    end
    if race ~= "Draco" then
        DracoNotify("Doi Race sang Draco truoc da")
        task.wait(5)
        return
    end
    if DracoCountItem("Primordial Reign") > 0 then
        DracoNotify("Done V3 Draco")
        task.wait(5)
        return
    end
    local wizard = DracoFindWizard()
    if not wizard then
        if not DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedNoWizard then
            DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedNoWizard = true
            DracoNotify("Khong thay Dragon Wizard - hay den Hydra Island (Sea 3)")
        end
        task.wait(2)
        return
    end
    DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedNoWizard = false
    local q = DracoUpgradeModule.Quest
    local state = q and q.AvailableVQuest or nil
    local inProgress = state == "V2InProgress" or state == "V3InProgress"
    local turnIn = state == "V2TurnInReady" or state == "V3TurnInReady"
    if not q or (not inProgress and not turnIn) then
        if DracoGoWizard(wizard) then return end
        local res = DracoSpeak()
        if type(res) == "table" and res.AvailableVQuest then
            DracoUpgradeModule.Quest = res
            if res.AvailableVQuest == "V2" or res.AvailableVQuest == "V3" then
                DracoAscension("Begin")
                task.wait(1)
                local res2 = DracoSpeak()
                if type(res2) == "table" and res2.AvailableVQuest then
                    DracoUpgradeModule.Quest = res2
                    if res2.AvailableVQuest == "V3InProgress" then
                        DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark = false
                    end
                end
            end
        else
            task.wait(10)
        end
        return
    end
    if turnIn then
        if DracoGoWizard(wizard) then return end
        DracoAscension("Complete")
        DracoUpgradeModule.Quest = nil
        task.wait(1)
        return
    end
    if state == "V2InProgress" then
        if DracoCountItem("Fire Flower") < 5 then
            local flower = DracoFindFireFlower()
            if flower then
                local target = flower.PrimaryPart or flower:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart")
                if target then
                    local hrp = DracoHRP()
                    if hrp and (target.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 8 then
                        DracoTween(target.CFrame)
                    else
                        pcall(function()
                            local prompt = flower:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("ProximityPrompt", true)
                            if prompt then fireproximityprompt(prompt, 1) end
                        end)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end
            else
                local mob = MobCheck and MobCheck("Forest Pirate") or nil
                if mob and mob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                    pcall(function()
                        if type(BringMob) == "function" then BringMob(mob) end
                    end)
                    DracoTween(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 20, 0))
                    pcall(function()
                        if type(Kill) == "function" then Kill(mob, true)
                        elseif Attack and Attack.Kill then Attack.Kill(mob, true) end
                    end)
                else
                    local tree = nil
                    pcall(function()
                        local wf = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map") and workspace.Map:FindFirstChild("Waterfall")
                        local im = wf and wf:FindFirstChild("IslandModel")
                        tree = im and im:FindFirstChild("Meshes/bambootree", true)
                    end)
                    if tree then DracoTween(tree.CFrame * CFrame.new(4, 0, 0)) end
                    task.wait(1)
                end
            end
        else
            if DracoGoWizard(wizard) then return end
            DracoAscension("Complete")
            DracoUpgradeModule.Quest = nil
            task.wait(1)
        end
        return
    end
    if state == "V3InProgress" then
        if DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark then
            if DracoGoWizard(wizard) then return end
            DracoAscension("Complete")
            DracoUpgradeModule.Quest = nil
            DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark = false
            task.wait(1)
            return
        end
        local shark = DracoFindShark()
        if shark then
            DracoUpgradeModule.SharkRef = shark
            local char = plr and plr.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if hum then hum.Sit = false end
            DracoTween(shark.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 25, 0))
            pcall(function()
                if type(Kill) == "function" then Kill(shark, true)
                elseif Attack and Attack.Kill then Attack.Kill(shark, true) end
            end)
            local h = shark:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
            if h and h.Health <= 0 then
                DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark = true
                DracoUpgradeModule.SharkRef = nil
            end
        else
            local ref = DracoUpgradeModule.SharkRef
            if ref then
                local h = ref:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                if not h or h.Health <= 0 then
                    DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark = true
                end
                DracoUpgradeModule.SharkRef = nil
            end
            if not DracoUpgradeModule.KilledTerrorshark then
                local boat, seat = DracoFindBoat()
                if not boat then
                    local dealer = CFrame.new(-16927.451, 9.086, 433.864)
                    local hrp = DracoHRP()
                    if hrp and (dealer.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 10 then
                        DracoTween(dealer)
                    else
                        pcall(function()
                            game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuyBoat", _G.SelectedBoat or "PirateBrigade")
                        end)
                        task.wait(1)
                    end
                else
                    local char = plr and plr.Character
                    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
                    if hum and not hum.Sit then
                        if seat then DracoTween(seat.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 1, 0)) end
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    else
                        local lv6 = CFrame.new(-44541.7617, 30.0003204, -1244.8584)
                        local hrp = DracoHRP()
                        if hrp and (lv6.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > 300 then
                            DracoTween(lv6)
                        end
                        task.wait(0.5)
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        return
    end
end

DracoToggle = v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Draco V2 & V3",
    Description = "Evolves Th Draco Race to V2 And V3",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        _G.AutoUpgradeRaceDraco = Value
        DracoUpgradeModule.NotifiedSea = false
        if Value then
            DracoUpgradeModule.Running = true
            task.spawn(function()
                while _G.AutoUpgradeRaceDraco do
                    pcall(function() DracoUpgradeModule.Step() end)
                    task.wait(0.5)
                end
                DracoUpgradeModule.Running = false
            end)
        else
            DracoUpgradeModule.Running = false
        end
    end
})

EliteQ = v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Elite Hunter",
    Description = "",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
    _G.FarmEliteHunt = Value
end})

spawn(function()
    while wait(1) do
        pcall(function()
            if _G.FarmEliteHunt then
                local questGui = plr.PlayerGui.Main.Quest
                local questTitle = questGui.Container.QuestTitle.Title.Text

                if not questGui.Visible then
                    
                    local result = replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("EliteHunter")
                    if result == nil or string.find(result, "Cooldown") then
                      
                        wait(10)
                        return
                    end
                    task.wait(1)
                else
                    
                    local eliteName = nil
                    for _, name in pairs({"Diablo", "Urban", "Deandre"}) do
                        if string.find(questTitle, name) then
                            eliteName = name
                            break
                        end
                    end

                    if eliteName then
                        local boss = nil
                        
                        for _, v in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
                            if v.Name == eliteName and v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                                boss = v
                                break
                            end
                        end
                        for _, v in pairs(Enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if v.Name == eliteName and Attack.Alive(v) then
                                boss = v
                                break
                            end
                        end

                        if boss and boss:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
                            _tp(boss.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
                            repeat
                                wait()
                                Attack.Kill(boss, _G.FarmEliteHunt)
                            until not _G.FarmEliteHunt or not boss.Parent or boss.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not questGui.Visible
                        else
                           
                            wait(5)
                        end
                    else
                       
                        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("AbandonQuest")
                    end
                end
            end
        end)
    end
end)

local mobList = {
    "Serpent Hunter",
    "Skull Slayer",
    "Isle Champion",
    "Sun-kissed Warrior"
}

-- Hàm bổ trợ lấy target chuẩn xác (Ưu tiên Boss -> Khóa target cũ -> Tìm quái gần nhất)
local function GetTyrantTarget(root)
    local enemies = workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies")
    if not enemies then return nil, false end

    -- 1. Ưu tiên hàng đầu: Boss Tyrant of the Skies
    local boss = enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies")
    if boss and boss:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and boss.Humanoid.Health > 0 and boss:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
        return boss, true
    end

    -- 2. Giữ nguyên quái đang đánh để tránh "nhảy bãi" liên tục
    if getgenv().CurrentTyrantTarget and getgenv().CurrentTyrantTarget.Parent then
        local target = getgenv().CurrentTyrantTarget
        local hum = target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        if hum and hum.Health > 0 and target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            return target, false
        end
    end

    -- 3. Tìm quái mồi gần nhất nếu chưa có target
    local closestMob = nil
    local closestDist = math.huge

    for _, mob in pairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
        local hum = mob:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
        local mobRoot = mob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

        if hum and mobRoot and hum.Health > 0 then
            for _, name in ipairs(mobList) do
                if mob.Name == name then
                    local dist = (mobRoot.Position - root.Position).Magnitude
                    if dist < closestDist then
                        closestDist = dist
                        closestMob = mob
                    end
                    break
                end
            end
        end
    end

    if closestMob then
        getgenv().CurrentTyrantTarget = closestMob
    end

    return closestMob, false
end

v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Tyrant of the Skies",
    Description = "",
    Default = false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        _G.FarmTyrant = Value

        if not Value then
            getgenv().CurrentTyrantTarget = nil
            return
        end

        task.spawn(function()
            while _G.FarmTyrant do
                task.wait(0.1)

                pcall(function()
                    local player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                    local character = player.Character or player.CharacterAdded:Wait()
                    local root = character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

                    if not root then return end

                    local target, isBoss = GetTyrantTarget(root)

                    if target and target:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and target:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
                        local mobRoot = target.HumanoidRootPart
                        local mobHum = target.Humanoid

                        -- Dịch chuyển đến vị trí đánh safe
                        local farmPos = mobRoot.CFrame * (FarmOffset and FarmOffset.Weapon or CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
                        if typeof(GetFarmStandCFrame) == "function" then
                            farmPos = GetFarmStandCFrame(target) or farmPos
                        end

                        if (root.Position - mobRoot.Position).Magnitude > 50 then
                            _tp(farmPos)
                        end

                        -- Vòng lặp dứt điểm target
                        repeat
                            task.wait()
                            if not _G.FarmTyrant then break end

                            if mobRoot and root then
                                local currentFarmPos = mobRoot.CFrame * (FarmOffset and FarmOffset.Weapon or CFrame.new(0, 30, 0))
                                _tp(currentFarmPos)
                            end

                            if Attack and Attack.Kill then
                                Attack.Kill(target, _G.FarmTyrant)
                            end
                        until not _G.FarmTyrant
                        or not target.Parent
                        or not mobHum
                        or mobHum.Health <= 0
                        -- Tự động ngắt farm quái mồi ngay lập tức nếu Boss xuất hiện
                        or (not isBoss and workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild("Tyrant of the Skies"))

                        if not target.Parent or mobHum.Health <= 0 then
                            getgenv().CurrentTyrantTarget = nil
                        end

                    else
                        -- Dịch chuyển về bãi đẻ quái/boss nếu chưa thấy target
                        local bossPos = CFrame.new(-16268.287, 152.616, 1390.773)
                        if (root.Position - bossPos.Position).Magnitude > 50 then
                            _tp(bossPos)
                        end
                    end
                end)
            end
        end)
    end
})



-- ========== HALLOWEEN EVENT FIXED - BAY 100% - TOC DO 199 - KHONG TELEPORT ==========
-- Fix 2 loi: teleport gay giat -> bay muot, toc bay trung binh 199
-- Link configs: FarmHeight, FarmDistance tu Settings tab

end -- Farm (Sea 3)
v487:AddSection({"Halloween Event [Fixed Bay 199]"})

-- Anti Gravity code ban gui - chi hoat dong khi bat Halloween
local function EnableAntiGravity_Halloween()
    pcall(function()
        local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if not char then return end
        local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then return end
        
        if hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGravityPVP") then
            hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGravityPVP"):Destroy()
        end
        
        local bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
        bv.Name = "AntiGravityPVP"
        bv.Parent = hrp
        bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
        
        if hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGyroPVP") then
            hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGyroPVP"):Destroy()
        end
        local bg = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
        bg.Name = "AntiGyroPVP"
        bg.Parent = hrp
        bg.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
        bg.P = 90000
        bg.CFrame = hrp.CFrame
        
        for _, part in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                part.CanCollide = false
            end
        end
        
        _G.AntiGravityEnabled_Halloween = true
        
        if _G.AntiGravityLoop_Halloween then
            pcall(function() _G.AntiGravityLoop_Halloween:Disconnect() end)
        end
        _G.AntiGravityLoop_Halloween = game:GetService("RunService").Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            pcall(function()
                if not _G.AntiGravityEnabled_Halloween then return end
                local c = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
                if not c then return end
                local h = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                local human = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                if not h then return end
                if human and human.Sit then human.Sit = false end
                local bv2 = h:FindFirstChild("AntiGravityPVP")
                if bv2 then bv2.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0) end
                if h.Anchored then h.Anchored = false end
            end)
        end)
    end)
end

local function DisableAntiGravity_Halloween()
    pcall(function()
        _G.AntiGravityEnabled_Halloween = false
        if _G.AntiGravityLoop_Halloween then
            _G.AntiGravityLoop_Halloween:Disconnect()
            _G.AntiGravityLoop_Halloween = nil
        end
        local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if char then
            local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hrp then
                if hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGravityPVP") then hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGravityPVP"):Destroy() end
                if hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGyroPVP") then hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGyroPVP"):Destroy() end
                hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0, -5, 0)
            end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                hum.Sit = false
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
            end
        end
    end)
end

-- BAY 100% - LINK CONFIGS SETTINGS - FARM GAN TANG TOC KHI <40 - TOC DO 199 - KHONG TELEPORT
local TweenService_Halloween = game:GetService("TweenService")
local CurrentTween_Halloween = nil

local function Halloween_FlyTo(targetCFrame, isBossSummon)
    if not targetCFrame or typeof(targetCFrame) ~= "CFrame" then return end
    pcall(function()
        local char = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        local hrp = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp then return end
        if hum and hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
        
        local distance = (hrp.Position - targetCFrame.Position).Magnitude
        
        -- LINK CONFIGS SETTINGS NHU CAC CHUC NANG KHAC 100%
        local farmHeight = getgenv().FarmHeight or _G.FarmHeight or getgenv().SafeFarmHeight or 15
        local farmDist = getgenv().FarmDistance or _G.FarmDistance or getgenv().SafeFarmDistance or 7
        
        -- LINK TOC DO BAY TU SETTINGS - GIONG CAC CHUC NANG KHAC
        local tweenNear = getgenv().TweenSpeedNear or _G.TweenSpeedNear or getgenv().TweenSpeed or 199
        local tweenFar = getgenv().TweenSpeedFar or _G.TweenSpeedFar or getgenv().TweenSpeed or 199
        
        -- FIX FARM GAN: phan biet gan/xa dung nhu cu - gan quai tang toc
        local speed = 199 -- mac dinh 199
        local isNear = distance < 150 -- gan quai
        local isVeryNear = distance < 40 -- rat gan quai <40 -> tang toc nhu cu
        
        if isBossSummon then
            -- Auto Summon + Farm Boss bay cham hon nhu cac chuc nang khac, khong bay nhanh qua
            if distance > 1000 then
                speed = tweenFar * 0.8 -- xa thi cham hon 20% de khong bay nhanh du
            else
                speed = tweenNear
            end
        else
            -- Cac chuc nang Halloween khac: Pumpkin Hunt, Snowman, TrickOrTreat
            if distance > 500 then
                speed = tweenFar -- xa dung Far
            else
                speed = tweenNear -- gan dung Near
            end
            -- Den gan quai khoang 40 thi tang toc nhu cu
            if isVeryNear then
                speed = speed * 1.8 -- tang toc 80% khi cach quai <40
            elseif isNear then
                speed = speed * 1.2 -- tang toc 20% khi cach <150
            end
        end
        
        -- Neu Settings chua set thi mac dinh 199
        if not getgenv().TweenSpeedNear and not _G.TweenSpeedNear and not getgenv().TweenSpeed and not getgenv().FarmDistance then
            speed = 199
        end
        
        if speed < 50 then speed = 50 end
        if speed > 400 then speed = 400 end
        
        local tweenTime = distance / speed
        if tweenTime < 0.2 then tweenTime = 0.2 end
        if tweenTime > 5 then tweenTime = 5 end
        
        if CurrentTween_Halloween then
            pcall(function() CurrentTween_Halloween:Cancel() end)
            CurrentTween_Halloween = nil
        end
        
        EnableAntiGravity_Halloween()
        
        local tweenInfo = TweenInfo.new(tweenTime, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear)
        CurrentTween_Halloween = TweenService_Halloween:Create(hrp, tweenInfo, {CFrame = targetCFrame})
        CurrentTween_Halloween:Play()
        
        local startTime = tick()
        repeat
            task.wait()
            if not _G.AntiGravityEnabled_Halloween then
                if CurrentTween_Halloween then CurrentTween_Halloween:Cancel() end
                break
            end
            if hum and hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
            local bv = hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGravityPVP")
            if bv then 
                bv.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
                bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9,9e9,9e9)
            end
            local bg = hrp:FindFirstChild("AntiGyroPVP")
            if bg then bg.CFrame = hrp.CFrame end
            if hrp.Anchored then hrp.Anchored = false end
        until not CurrentTween_Halloween or CurrentTween_Halloween.PlaybackState == Enum.PlaybackState.Completed or tick() - startTime > tweenTime + 2
    end)
end

-- Helpers
local function GetHRP_Halloween() 
    local ch = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
    return ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end
local function PivotOf_Halloween(o)
    if not o or not o.Parent then return nil end
    if o:IsA("Model") then return o:GetPivot()
    elseif o:IsA("BasePart") then return o.CFrame end
    return nil
end
local function IsMobAlive_Halloween(m)
    if not m or not m.Parent then return false end
    local hum = m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = m:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hum or not hrp then return false end
    return hum.Health > 0
end
local function CollectPumpkins_Halloween()
    local list = {}
    local root = workspace:FindFirstChild("PumpkinHunt")
    if not root then return list end
    for _, c in ipairs(root:GetChildren()) do
        if c:IsA("Model") or c:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(list,c)
        elseif c:IsA("Folder") then
            for _, cc in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                if cc:IsA("Model") or cc:IsA("BasePart") then table.insert(list,cc) end
            end
        end
    end
    return list
end

-- Configs
getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt = getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt or false
getgenv().KillAuraJackOLantern = getgenv().KillAuraJackOLantern or false
getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman = getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman or false
getgenv().AutoHopPumpkinPatch = getgenv().AutoHopPumpkinPatch or false
getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat = getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat or false
getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss = getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss or false

v487:AddParagraph({Title = "Testing", Content = "Link 100% configs Settings nhu cac chuc nang khac: FarmHeight, FarmDistance, TweenSpeedNear, TweenSpeedFar - Mac dinh toc bay 199"})

v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Farm Pumpkin Hunt",
    Description = "Link configs Settings nhu chuc nang khac: FarmHeight, FarmDistance, TweenSpeedNear/Far - Toc 199",
    Default = getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt or false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt = Value
        if Value then EnableAntiGravity_Halloween() else
            if not (getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman or getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat or getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss) then DisableAntiGravity_Halloween() end
        end
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                local JACK_RANGE = 800
                local function normName(s) return string.lower(tostring(s or "")):gsub("[^%w]","") end
                local function findJackMob()
                    local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                    if not hrp then return nil end
                    local best, bestD = nil, JACK_RANGE
                    local enemies = workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies")
                    if enemies then
                        for _, m in ipairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                            if (normName(m.Name)=="jackolantern" or normName(m.Name)=="pumpkinsnowman") and IsMobAlive_Halloween(m) then
                                local d = (m.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                                if d <= bestD then best, bestD = m, d end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return best
                end
                local function nearestPumpkin()
                    local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                    if not hrp then return nil end
                    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
                    for _, p in ipairs(CollectPumpkins_Halloween()) do
                        local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(p)
                        if cf then
                            local d = (cf.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if d < bestD then best, bestD = p, d end
                        end
                    end
                    return best
                end
                while getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt and task.wait(0.1) do
                    pcall(function()
                        local mob = findJackMob()
                        if mob then
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                if not getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt then return end
                                local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                                if not hrp or not mob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (mob.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > JACK_RANGE then return end
                                local h = getgenv().FarmHeight or _G.FarmHeight or 15
                                local d = getgenv().FarmDistance or _G.FarmDistance or 7
                                Halloween_FlyTo(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(d, h, 0))
                            until not IsMobAlive_Halloween(mob)
                        else
                            local p = nearestPumpkin()
                            if p then
                                local startT = tick()
                                local standDir
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    if not getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt then return end
                                    local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                                    local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(p)
                                    if not hrp or not cf then return end
                                    if not standDir then
                                        local dir = hrp.Position - cf.Position
                                        dir = Vector3.new(dir.X,0,dir.Z)
                                        standDir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0,0,1)
                                    end
                                    local stand = CFrame.new(cf.Position + standDir * 5)
                                    if (hrp.Position - stand.Position).Magnitude <= 3 then
                                        Halloween_FlyTo(hrp.CFrame)
                                    else
                                        Halloween_FlyTo(stand)
                                    end
                                until not p.Parent or tick() - startT > 12
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})

v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Kill Aura Jack O' Lantern",
    Default = getgenv().KillAuraJackOLantern or false,
    Callback = function(Value) getgenv().KillAuraJackOLantern = Value end
})

v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Farm Pumpkin Snowman",
    Description = "",
    Default = getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman or false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman = Value
        if Value then EnableAntiGravity_Halloween() else
            if not (getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt or getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat or getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss) then DisableAntiGravity_Halloween() end
        end
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                local JACK_RANGE = 800
                local function normName(s) return string.lower(tostring(s or "")):gsub("[^%w]","") end
                local function findPatch()
                    local root = workspace:FindFirstChild("PumpkinHunt")
                    local p = root and root:FindFirstChild("PumpkinPatch")
                    if p and PivotOf_Halloween(p) then return p end
                    return nil
                end
                local function findSnowMob()
                    local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                    local enemies = workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies")
                    if not hrp or not enemies then return nil end
                    local best, bestD = nil, JACK_RANGE
                    for _, m in ipairs(enemies:GetChildren()) do
                        if normName(m.Name)=="pumpkinsnowman" and IsMobAlive_Halloween(m) then
                            local d = (m.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                            if d <= bestD then best, bestD = m, d end
                        end
                    end
                    return best
                end
                while getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman and task.wait(0.1) do
                    pcall(function()
                        local mob = findSnowMob()
                        if mob then
                            repeat
                                task.wait()
                                if not getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman then return end
                                local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                                if not hrp or not mob:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or (mob.HumanoidRootPart.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude > JACK_RANGE then return end
                                local h = getgenv().FarmHeight or _G.FarmHeight or 15
                                local d = getgenv().FarmDistance or _G.FarmDistance or 7
                                Halloween_FlyTo(mob.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(d, h, 0))
                            until not IsMobAlive_Halloween(mob)
                        else
                            local patch = findPatch()
                            if patch then
                                local startT = tick()
                                local standDir
                                repeat
                                    task.wait()
                                    if not getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman then return end
                                    if findSnowMob() then return end
                                    local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                                    local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(patch)
                                    if not hrp or not cf then return end
                                    if not standDir then
                                        local dir = hrp.Position - cf.Position
                                        dir = Vector3.new(dir.X,0,dir.Z)
                                        standDir = dir.Magnitude > 0.1 and dir.Unit or Vector3.new(0,0,1)
                                    end
                                    local stand = CFrame.new(cf.Position + standDir * 5)
                                    if (hrp.Position - stand.Position).Magnitude <= 3 then
                                        Halloween_FlyTo(hrp.CFrame)
                                    else
                                        Halloween_FlyTo(stand)
                                    end
                                until not patch.Parent or tick() - startT > 12
                            end
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})



v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Trick Or Treat",
    Default = getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat or false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat = Value
        if Value then EnableAntiGravity_Halloween() else
            if not (getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt or getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman or getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss) then DisableAntiGravity_Halloween() end
        end
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                local TT_REVISIT = 120
                local ttVisited = {}
                local function collectDoors()
                    local list = {}
                    local map = workspace:FindFirstChild("Map")
                    if not map then return list end
                    for _, island in ipairs(map:GetChildren()) do
                        local decor = island:FindFirstChild("HalloweenDecor")
                        if decor then
                            for _, d in ipairs(decor:GetDescendants()) do
                                if d.Name=="HalloweenDoor" and (d:IsA("Model") or d:IsA("BasePart")) then table.insert(list,d) end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return list
                end
                local function nearestDoor()
                    local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                    if not hrp then return nil end
                    local best, bestD = nil, math.huge
                    for _, d in ipairs(collectDoors()) do
                        if (ttVisited[d] or 0) < tick() then
                            local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(d)
                            if cf then
                                local dist = (cf.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
                                if dist < bestD then best, bestD = d, dist end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                    return best
                end
                while getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat and task.wait(0.1) do
                    pcall(function()
                        local minute = tonumber(os.date("%M")) or 0
                        if minute >= 10 then task.wait(1) return end
                        local ghostRoot = workspace:FindFirstChild("GhostChase")
                        if ghostRoot and #ghostRoot:GetChildren() > 0 then
                            for _, c in ipairs(ghostRoot:GetChildren()) do
                                if not getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat then return end
                                local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(c)
                                if cf then Halloween_FlyTo(CFrame.new(cf.Position)) task.wait(0.2) end
                            end
                            return
                        end
                        local door = nearestDoor()
                        if not door then task.wait(2) return end
                        local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(door)
                        if not cf then return end
                        Halloween_FlyTo(CFrame.new(cf.Position + Vector3.new(0,0,3)))
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                        vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                        task.wait(0.5)
                        vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                        task.wait(1)
                        local pg = game.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
                        local dg = pg and pg:FindFirstChild("DialogueGui")
                        if dg then
                            for _, ol in ipairs(dg:GetDescendants()) do
                                if ol.Name=="optionsList" then
                                    local sc = ol:FindFirstChild("scroller")
                                    if sc then
                                        for _, o in ipairs(sc:GetChildren()) do
                                            if o:IsA("GuiObject") and string.find(o.Name,"option1",1,true) and o.Visible then
                                                local pos = o.AbsolutePosition + o.AbsoluteSize/2
                                                vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, 0, true, game, 1)
                                                vim:SendMouseButtonEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, 0, false, game, 1)
                                                task.wait(0.5)
                                            end
                                        end
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                        ttVisited[door] = tick() + TT_REVISIT
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})

v487:AddToggle({
    Name = "Auto Summon + Farm Boss",
    Description = "",
    Default = getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss or false,
    Callback = function(Value)
        getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss = Value
        if Value then EnableAntiGravity_Halloween() else
            if not (getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinHunt or getgenv().AutoFarmPumpkinSnowman or getgenv().AutoTrickOrTreat) then DisableAntiGravity_Halloween() end
        end
        if Value then
            task.spawn(function()
                while getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss and task.wait(0.1) do
                    pcall(function()
                        local hrp = GetHRP_Halloween()
                        if not hrp then return end
                        local npcs = workspace:FindFirstChild("NPCs")
                        local witch = npcs and npcs:FindFirstChild("Witch")
                        local compass = workspace:FindFirstChild("GreatPumpkinCompass")
                        local enemies = workspace:FindFirstChild("Enemies")
                        local dapper = enemies and enemies:FindFirstChild("Dapper Jack")
                        local hellfire = enemies and enemies:FindFirstChild("Hellfire Gas")
                        local h = getgenv().FarmHeight or _G.FarmHeight or 15
                        local d = getgenv().FarmDistance or _G.FarmDistance or 7
                        if hellfire and IsMobAlive_Halloween(hellfire) then
                            Halloween_FlyTo(hellfire.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(d, h, 0), true)
                            return
                        end
                        if dapper and IsMobAlive_Halloween(dapper) then
                            Halloween_FlyTo(dapper.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(d, h, 0), true)
                            return
                        end
                        if compass and PivotOf_Halloween(compass) then
                            local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(compass)
                            Halloween_FlyTo(CFrame.new(cf.Position), true)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                            local vim = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
                            vim:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                            task.wait(1)
                            vim:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
                            task.wait(5)
                            return
                        end
                        if witch and PivotOf_Halloween(witch) then
                            local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(witch)
                            Halloween_FlyTo(CFrame.new(cf.Position), true)
                            task.wait(0.5)
                            pcall(function()
                                game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage").Remotes.DapperJackRitual:InvokeServer("Summon")
                            end)
                            task.wait(15)
                            return
                        else
                            local islandCF
                            if game.PlaceId == 4442272183 then islandCF = CFrame.new(-703, 9, 1575)
                            elseif game.PlaceId == 7449423635 then islandCF = CFrame.new(-385, 73, 297)
                            else islandCF = CFrame.new(-12548, 337, -7481) end
                            Halloween_FlyTo(islandCF, true)
                        end
                        local candyRoot = workspace:FindFirstChild("CandySpillFx")
                        if candyRoot and #candyRoot:GetChildren() > 0 then
                            for _, c in ipairs(candyRoot:GetChildren()) do
                                if not getgenv().AutoSummonFarmBoss then return end
                                local cf = PivotOf_Halloween(c)
                                if cf then Halloween_FlyTo(CFrame.new(cf.Position), true) task.wait(0.3) end
                            end
                            local lp = game.Players.LocalPlayer
                            local hum = lp.Character and lp.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                            if hum then hum.Health = 0 end
                            task.wait(2)
                        end
                    end)
                end
            end)
        end
    end
})

Default = false,

spawn(function()
  while wait(Sec) do

            end

	    end

if World3 then -- Auto Fish Slap (Sea 3)
v487:AddSection({"Auto Fish Slap"})

_G.MiNigame = v487:AddToggle({
  	Name="Auto Fish Slap",
	  Default=false,
  	Callback=function(v) _G.MiNigame=v end
 })

Players=game:GetService("Players")
RS=game:GetService("RunService")
LP=Players.LocalPlayer

local function getFishUI()
	local pg=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui"); if not pg then return end
	local g=pg:FindFirstChild("FishSlapMinigame"); if not g then return end
	local bar=g:FindFirstChild("Bar"); if not bar then return end
	return bar:FindFirstChild("GreenZone"), bar:FindFirstChild("Tick"), g:FindFirstChild("SlapButton")
end

local function getContinueBtn()
	local pg=LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui"); if not pg then return end
	local cs=pg:FindFirstChild("ContinueScreen"); if not cs then return end
	return cs:FindFirstChild("Continue")
end

local function rect(o)
	if not (o and o.AbsoluteSize) then return end
	local p,s=o.AbsolutePosition,o.AbsoluteSize
	return p.X,p.Y,p.X+s.X,p.Y+s.Y
end

local function overlap(a,b)
	local ax1,ay1,ax2,ay2=rect(a); local bx1,by1,bx2,by2=rect(b)
	if not (ax1 and bx1) then return false end
	return ax1<bx2 and ax2>bx1 and ay1<by2 and ay2>by1
end

local function fire(btn)
	if not btn then return false end
	local ok=false
	pcall(function()
		if firesignal then
			local ev=btn.Activated or btn.MouseButton1Click
			if ev then firesignal(ev); ok=true end
		end
	end)
	if not ok then pcall(function() btn:Activate(); ok=true end) end
	return ok
end

if _G.__FishSlapConn then pcall(function() _G.__FishSlapConn:Disconnect() end) end

last,lastC,prev=0,0,false
_G.__FishSlapConn=RS.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
	if not _G.MiNigame then prev=false return end

	local gz,tk,btn=getFishUI()
	if gz and tk and btn then
		local over=overlap(tk,gz)
		local now=tick()
		if over and not prev and now-last>=0.03 then
			if fire(btn) then last=now end
		end
		prev=over
	else
		prev=false
	end

	local c=getContinueBtn()
	if c and c.Visible and tick()-lastC>=0.25 then
		if fire(c) then lastC=tick() end
	end
end)


end -- Auto Fish Slap (Sea 3)
v487:AddSection({"Auto Raid Evens Fruits"})

    if _G.AutoRaidCastle then
      pcall(function()
      local CFrameCastleRaid = CFrame.new(-5496.17432, 313.768921, -2841.53027, 0.924894512, 7.37058015e-09, 0.380223751, 3.5881019e-08, 1, -1.06665446e-07, -0.380223751, 1.12297109e-07, 0.924894512)
        if (CFrame.new(-5539.3115234375, 313.800537109375, -2972.372314453125).Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 500 then
          for i,v in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
            if v:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and v:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") and v.Humanoid.Health > 0 then
              if v.Name then
                if (v.HumanoidRootPart.Position - Root.Position).Magnitude <= 2000 then
                  repeat wait() Attack.Kill(v,_G.AutoRaidCastle) until not _G.AutoRaidCastle or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or not workspace.Enemies:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
                end
              end
            end
          end
        else
          local Castle_Mob = {"Galley Pirate","Galley Captain","Raider","Mercenary","Vampire","Zombie","Snow Trooper","Winter Warrior","Lab Subordinate","Horned Warrior","Magma Ninja","Lava Pirate","Ship Deckhand","Ship Engineer","Ship Steward","Ship Officer","Arctic Warrior","Snow Lurker","Sea Soldier","Water Fighter"}
          for i = 1,#Castle_Mob do
            if replicated:FindFirstChild(Castle_Mob[i]) then
              for _,v in pairs(replicated:GetChildren()) do
                if table.find(Castle_Mob, v.Name) then _tp(CFrameCastleRaid) end
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end)
    end
  end
end)

          v.Head.CanCollide = false
          v.Humanoid.Sit = false
          v.HumanoidRootPart.CanCollide = false
          v.Humanoid.JumpPower = 0

          if v.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator') then v.Humanoid:FindFirstChild('Animator'):Destroy() end
        end    
      end)
    end
  end
end)
function getT(num)
    local rotation
    if num == 1 then
        rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment1.Line.Rotation
    elseif num == 3 then
        rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment3.Line.Rotation
    elseif num == 4 then
        rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment4.Line.Rotation
    elseif num == 7 then
        rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment7.Line.Rotation
    elseif num == 10 then
        rotation = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Tablet.Segment10.Line.Rotation
    end
    if rotation then
        return rotation.Z
    end
end
function getRT(num)
    local Trophy_Q = workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"].Trophies.Quest
    local Trophy_Pos
    for _, v in pairs(Trophy_Q:GetChildren()) do
        if num == 1 and v.Name == "Trophy1" and v:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            Trophy_Pos = v.Handle.Rotation
        elseif num == 2 and v.Name == "Trophy2" and v:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            Trophy_Pos = v.Handle.Rotation         
        elseif num == 3 and v.Name == "Trophy3" and v:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            Trophy_Pos = v.Handle.Rotation       
        elseif num == 4 and v.Name == "Trophy4" and v:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            Trophy_Pos = v.Handle.Rotation  
        elseif num == 5 and v.Name == "Trophy5" and v:FindFirstChild("Handle") then
            Trophy_Pos = v.Handle.Rotation     
        end          
        if Trophy_Pos then
            return Trophy_Pos.Z   
        end
    end
end
GetFirePlacard = function(Number,Side)
  if tostring(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"]["Placard"..Number][Side].Indicator.BrickColor) ~= "Pearl" then
    fireclickdetector(workspace.Map["Haunted Castle"]["Placard"..Number][Side].ClickDetector)
  end
end

          for _, v in next, replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory") do          
            if type(v) == "table" then
              if v.Type == "Sword" then
                SwordName = v.Name
                if tonumber(v.Mastery) >= 1 or tonumber(v.Mastery) <= 599 then
                  local v = GetConnectionEnemies(mastery1)
                  if GetBP(SwordName) then                    
		            if v then
                      repeat wait() Attack.Sword(v,_G.FarmMastery_S) until _G.FarmMastery_S == false or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0		                  
		            else
		              _tp(CFrame.new(-1943.676513671875, 251.5095672607422, -12337.880859375)) 
		            end                    
                  else
                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem",SwordName)   
                  end   
              elseif tonumber(v.Mastery) >= 600 then
                if GetBP(SwordName) then return nil else replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem",SwordName) end       
              end
                break
              end
            end         
          end
        elseif SelectIsland == "Bone" then
          for _, v in next, replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("getInventory") do          
            if type(v) == "table" then
              if v.Type == "Sword" then
                SwordName = v.Name
                if tonumber(v.Mastery) >= 1 or tonumber(v.Mastery) <= 599 then
                  local v = GetConnectionEnemies(mastery2)
                  if GetBP(SwordName) then                    
		            if v then
                      repeat wait() Attack.Sword(v,_G.FarmMastery_S) until _G.FarmMastery_S == false or not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0		                  
		            else
		              _tp(CFrame.new(-9495.6806640625, 453.58624267578125, 5977.3486328125)) 
		            end                    
                  else
                    replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem",SwordName)   
                  end   
                elseif tonumber(v.Mastery) >= 600 then
                  if GetBP(SwordName) then return nil else replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadItem",SwordName) end       
                end
                break
              end
            end         
          end
        end
      end
    end)
  end
end)

Q = v487:AddToggle({
Name = "Auto Unlocked Don Swan", 
Description = "", 
Default = false,
Callback = function(Value)
  _G.Auto_DonAcces = Value
end})
spawn(function()
  while wait(.1) do
    if _G.Auto_DonAcces then
      pcall(function()
        if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil and plr.Data.Level.Value >= 1500 then
          FruitPrice = {}
	      FruitStore = {}
		  for i,v in next,replicated:WaitForChild("Remotes").CommF_:InvokeServer("GetFruits") do
		    if v.Price >= 1000000 then  
		     table.insert(FruitPrice,v.Name)
		    end
		  end
		  for i,v in pairs(replicated.Remotes["CommF_"]:InvokeServer("getInventoryFruits")) do
		    for _,x in pairs(v) do
		      if _ == "Name" then 
		        table.insert(FruitStore,x)
		      end
	        end
	          replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("Cousin","Buy")
	          for _,y in pairs(FruitPrice) do
		        for _,z in pairs(FruitStore) do
		          if y == z and replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess == nil then
		            _G.StoreF = false
			      if not plr.Backpack:FindFirstChild(FruitStore) then
			        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("LoadFruit",tostring(y))
			      else
			        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor","1")
			        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor","2")
			        replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("TalkTrevor","3")
			      end
			    end
		      end 
		    end
		    if replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("GetUnlockables").FlamingoAccess ~= nil then
		      _G.StoreF = true
		      _G.Auto_DonAcces = false
		    end

			        repeat wait() _tp(CFrame.new(-2604.6958, 239.432526, -10315.1982, 0.0425701365, 0, -0.999093413, 0, 1, 0, 0.999093413, 0, 0.0425701365)) until not _G.Auto_SharkMan_Karate or (Root.Position == CFrame.new(-2604.6958, 239.432526, -10315.1982, 0.0425701365, 0, -0.999093413, 0, 1, 0, 0.999093413, 0, 0.0425701365).Position)
	                replicated.Remotes.CommF_:InvokeServer("BuySharkmanKarate")
		          end
		        end
		      elseif not GetBP("Water Key") then
		        local v = GetConnectionEnemies("Tide Keeper")
		        if v then repeat wait() Attack.Kill(v,_G.Auto_SharkMan_Karate)until not v.Parent or v.Humanoid.Health <= 0 or _G.Auto_SharkMan_Karate == false or GetBP("Water Key") or GetBP("Sharkman Karate")		
	            else _tp(CFrame.new(-3053.9814453125, 237.18954467773, -10145.0390625))
	            end
		      end		                  
            end          
          end
        end
      end)
    end
  end
end)

-- ========== KURRI COMPAT SHIM + MODULE (lien ket Misc + Sea) ==========
-- Cac bien Kurri dung nhung Hu999 khong co - dinh nghia o day de khong loi nil
-- [KURI] Settings GOC cua Kurri (lay nguyen tu ban Kurri): doc-ghi file that, khong phai shim fake.
-- Truoc day _G.SaveData luon = {} moi lan chay nen Misc khong luu duoc setting.
if not _G.SaveData then _G.SaveData = {} end
if not SaveSettings then
    SaveSettings = function()
        if writefile then
            local ok, json = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(_G.SaveData) end)
            if ok then pcall(function() if makefolder and not isfolder("Redz Hub - OK Hub") then makefolder("Redz Hub - OK Hub") end writefile("Redz Hub - OK Hub/Configs.json", json) end) end
        end
    end
end
if not LoadSettings then
    LoadSettings = function()
        if isfile and isfile("Redz Hub - OK Hub/Configs.json") then
            local json = readfile("Redz Hub - OK Hub/Configs.json")
            local ok, saveData = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(json) end)
            if ok and type(saveData) == "table" then _G.SaveData = saveData return true end
        end
        return false
    end
end
if not GetSetting then
    GetSetting = function(k, d) if _G.SaveData[k] == nil then return d end return _G.SaveData[k] end
end
if not _G._KurriSettingsLoaded then _G._KurriSettingsLoaded = true pcall(LoadSettings) end
if not FolderName then FolderName = "Redz Hub - OK Hub" end
if not Player then Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer end
if not LocalPlayer3 then LocalPlayer3 = game.Players.LocalPlayer end
if not ReplicatedStorage6 then ReplicatedStorage6 = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end
if not RunService6 then RunService6 = game:GetService("RunService") end
if not KurriRS then KurriRS = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage") end
if not Sec then Sec = 0.1 end
if not rejoinConnection then rejoinConnection = nil end
if not AutoRejoinKick then AutoRejoinKick = true end
-- KurriTween: tween nhan vat toi CFrame (Kurri dung tween(CFrame), Hu999 khong co ham nay)
if not KurriTween then
    KurriTween = function(target)
        if getgenv().TweenPaused then return end
        if typeof(target) ~= "CFrame" then return end
        local c = game.Players.LocalPlayer.Character
        if not c then return end
        local hrp = c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not hrp or not hum then return end
        pcall(function()
            local dist = (hrp.Position - target.Position).Magnitude
            local speed = _G.TweenSpeed or getgenv().TweenSpeed or 300
            local dur = math.max(0.1, dist / math.max(1, speed))
            local tw = game:GetService("TweenService"):Create(hrp, TweenInfo.new(dur, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear), {CFrame = target})
            tw:Play()
            -- Cho toi khi toi noi hoac bi tat
            local t0 = tick()
            while tick() - t0 < dur + 2 do
                task.wait(0.1)
                if getgenv().TweenPaused then tw:Cancel() break end
                if (hrp.Position - target.Position).Magnitude < 15 then break end
            end
        end)
    end
end
-- SeaModule + MiscModule: de chuc nang chay that, lien ket voi _G
local SeaModule = {}
_G.SeaModule = SeaModule; getgenv().SeaModule = SeaModule
SeaModule.IsSeaEvent = function()
    local ok, r = pcall(function() return IsDoingSeaEvent and IsDoingSeaEvent() or false end)
    return ok and r or false
end
local MiscModule = {}
_G.MiscModule = MiscModule; getgenv().MiscModule = MiscModule
MiscModule.Hop = function()
    pcall(function()
        local HS = game:GetService("HttpService"); local TS = game:GetService("TeleportService")
        local servers = HS:JSONDecode(game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/"..game.PlaceId.."/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100"))
        for _, s in pairs(servers.data) do if s.playing < s.maxPlayers and s.id ~= game.JobId then TS:TeleportToPlaceInstance(game.PlaceId, s.id) break end end
    end)
end
MiscModule.Rejoin = function() pcall(function() game:GetService("TeleportService"):Teleport(game.PlaceId, game.Players.LocalPlayer) end) end
-- Loop lien ket: Misc + Sea doc _G/getgenv, ap dung NoClip / Speed / WalkWater / AntiBan
if not _G._KurriLinkLoop then
    _G._KurriLinkLoop = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while task.wait(0.25) do
            pcall(function()
                -- NoClip do NoClip Manager lo (tat het farm -> tra va cham)
                if _G.AntiBand then
                    for _, pl in pairs(game:GetService("Players"):GetPlayers()) do
                        if table.find({17884881,120173604,912348}, pl.UserId) then MiscModule.Hop() break end
                    end
                end
            end)
        end
    end)
end

	local n15 = 0
	local n16 = 0
	local n17 = 1

	GetWeaponList = function()
		local t35 = {}

		if _G.UseMelee then
			t35[#t35 + 1] = "Melee"
		end

		if _G.UseSword then
			t35[#t35 + 1] = "Sword"
		end

		if _G.UseGun then
			t35[#t35 + 1] = "Gun"
		end

		if _G.UseFruit then
			t35[#t35 + 1] = "Blox Fruit"
		end

		return t35
	end
	SwitchWeapon = function()
		if tick() - n16 < NoSkid then
			return
		end

		n16 = tick()

		local v1101 = GetWeaponList()

		if #v1101 == 0 then
			return
		end

		n17 = n17 % #v1101 + 1
		weaponSc(v1101[n17])
	end
	SpamSkill = function()
		if tick() - n15 < HNCU then
			return
		end

		n15 = tick()

		local v1102 = GetWeaponList()

		if #v1102 == 0 then
			return
		end

		local v1103 = v1102[n17]

		weaponSc(v1103)
		task.wait()

		if v1103 == "Melee" then
			if _G.MeleeZ then
				_S(v1103, "Z")
			end

			if _G.MeleeX then
				_S(v1103, "X")
			end

			if _G.MeleeC then
				_S(v1103, "C")
			end
		elseif v1103 == "Sword" then
			if _G.SwordZ then
				_S(v1103, "Z")
			end

			if _G.SwordX then
				_S(v1103, "X")
			end
		elseif v1103 == "Blox Fruit" then
			if _G.BloxFruitZ then
				_S(v1103, "Z")
			end

			if _G.BloxFruitX then
				_S(v1103, "X")
			end

			if _G.BloxFruitC then
				_S(v1103, "C")
			end

			if _G.BloxFruitV then
				_S(v1103, "V")
			end

			if _G.BloxFruitF then
				_S(v1103, "F")
			end
		elseif v1103 == "Gun" then
			if _G.GunZ then
				_S(v1103, "Z")
			end

			if _G.GunX then
				_S(v1103, "X")
			end
		end
	end

		while wait() do
			pcall(function()
				if _G.Shark and CheckShark() then

								if not _G.Shark then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.TerrorShark and CheckTerrorShark() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "Terrorshark" and t8.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								KurriTween(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
								KillSea(child, _G.TerrorShark)

								if not _G.TerrorShark then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.Piranha then
					if CheckPiranha() then
						for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
							if child.Name == "Piranha" and t8.Alive(child) then
								while true do
									task.wait()
									KurriTween(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
									KillSea(child, _G.Piranha)

									if not _G.Piranha then
										break
									elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.MobCrew and CheckFishCrew() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "Fish Crew Member" and t8.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								KurriTween(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
								KillSea(child, _G.MobCrew)

								if not _G.MobCrew then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.HCM and CheckHauntedCrew() then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child.Name == "Haunted Crew Member" and t8.Alive(child) then
							while true do
								task.wait()
								KurriTween(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))
								KillSea(child, _G.HCM)

								if not _G.HCM then
									break
								elseif not child.Parent or child.Humanoid.Health <= 0 then
									break
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.seabeatfarm then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.SeaBeasts:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								KurriTween(CFrame.new(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.X, workspace.Map["WaterBase-Plane"].Position.Y + 200, child.HumanoidRootPart.Position.Z))

								if LocalPlayer8:DistanceFromCharacter(child.HumanoidRootPart.Position) <= 500 then
									MousePos = child.HumanoidRootPart.Position
									SpamSkill()
								end
							until not _G.seabeatfarm or not child.Parent or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.FishBoat then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
							repeat
								task.wait()
								KurriTween(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -50, -25))

								if LocalPlayer8:DistanceFromCharacter(child.Engine.Position) <= 150 then
									MousePos = child.Engine.Position
									SpamSkill()
								end
							until not _G.FishBoat or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end

				if _G.PGB then
					for _, child in pairs(workspace.Enemies:GetChildren()) do
						if child:FindFirstChild("VehicleSeat") and child:FindFirstChild("Health") and child.Health.Value > 0 then
							repeat
								task.wait()

								if child.Name == "PirateBrigade" then
									KurriTween(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -30, -10))
								else
									KurriTween(child.Engine.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, -50, -50))
								end

								if LocalPlayer8:DistanceFromCharacter(child.Engine.Position) <= 150 then
									MousePos = child.Engine.Position
									SpamSkill()
								end
							until not _G.PGB or child.Health.Value <= 0
						end
					end
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
end
local _okSea, _errSea = pcall(_SeaKurri)
if not _okSea then warn('[Sea Kurri] '..tostring(_errSea)) end
